#### API-23 执行试算平衡

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m17/ledgers/trial-balance
```

```json
{
  "period": "2026-10",
  "round_no": null
}
```

**响应（平衡）**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "period": "2026-10",
    "round_no": 1,
    "is_balanced": true,
    "subject_count": 1842,
    "voucher_count": 5052,
    "entry_count": 35364,
    "total_open_debit": 12480000.00,
    "total_open_credit": 12480000.00,
    "total_period_debit": 8620450.00,
    "total_period_credit": 8620450.00,
    "total_end_debit": 15320700.00,
    "total_end_credit": 15320700.00,
    "open_diff": 0.00,
    "period_diff": 0.00,
    "end_diff": 0.00,
    "unbalanced_subjects": null,
    "check_at": "2026-10-31T18:02:15",
    "elapsed_ms": 428
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**响应（不平衡）**

```json
{
  "code": "1705012",
  "message": "试算不平衡：本期发生差额 0.01",
  "data": {
    "period": "2026-10",
    "round_no": 2,
    "is_balanced": false,
    "subject_count": 1842,
    "total_period_debit": 8620450.01,
    "total_period_credit": 8620450.00,
    "period_diff": 0.01,
    "unbalanced_subjects": [
      {"subject_code": "6602", "subject_name": "财务费用", "diff": 0.01, "side": "DEBIT"}
    ],
    "suggestions": [
      "检查本期凭证是否有绕过系统直接改数（查审计日志 is_financial_impact = TRUE）",
      "使用账证核对比对明细账发生额与凭证分录汇总",
      "检查是否存在以非末级科目记账的凭证"
    ]
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验期间存在且可试算（已结账期间允许重算，用于复核）；2) 计算三组合计（`SUM(open_balance)` / `SUM(period_debit)` / `SUM(end_balance)` 分借贷方向）；3) 计算三项差额；4) `round_no` 自动递增（同一期间多轮试算全部留存）；5) **不平衡时定位差异科目**：按科目聚合 `period_debit - period_credit`，找出非零科目；6) 落库 `m17_trial_balance`（条件式 CHECK：`is_balanced = TRUE` 时才强制合计相等，不平衡时允许落库留证）；7) 不平衡返回 `"1705012"` 但**仍返回完整差异数据**（财务需要看到「差多少、差在哪」）；8) 发事件（供 P08 结账判定使用）。

**关键设计：** 不平衡时返回 `"1705012"` 而非 `0`，但 `data` 中仍带完整结果——**报错不等于无数据**。这与「试算平衡本身是检验动作，可能得出不平衡的结论」一致。

**错误码：** `"1705012"`、`"1705003"`、`"179001"`、`"1705038"`。

#### API-24 结账预检

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m17/ledgers/close/precheck?period=2026-10
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "period": "2026-10",
    "can_close": true,
    "block_reasons": [],
    "trial_balanced": true,
    "total_debit": 8620450.00,
    "total_credit": 8620450.00,
    "voucher_count": 5213,
    "unposted_count": 0,
    "draft_count": 2,
    "warn_count": 3,
    "fail_count": 0,
    "checks": [
      {
        "check_code": "VOUCHER_UNPOSTED",
        "check_name": "存在未过账凭证",
        "check_category": "COMPLETENESS",
        "severity": "FAIL",
        "check_result": "PASS",
        "check_value": "0",
        "expected_value": "0",
        "diff_value": null,
        "problem_count": 0,
        "detail_json": null,
        "is_overridden": false,
        "override_approval_no": null
      },
      {
        "check_code": "VOUCHER_DRAFT",
        "check_name": "存在未提交草稿",
        "check_category": "COMPLETENESS",
        "severity": "WARN",
        "check_result": "WARN",
        "check_value": "2",
        "expected_value": "0",
        "diff_value": null,
        "problem_count": 2,
        "detail_json": [
          {"voucher_no": "V202610-0521", "status": "DRAFT", "summary": "补提折旧"}
        ],
        "is_overridden": false,
        "override_approval_no": null
      },
      {
        "check_code": "TRIAL_BALANCE",
        "check_name": "试算不平衡",
        "check_category": "BALANCE",
        "severity": "FAIL",
        "check_result": "PASS",
        "check_value": "差额 0.00",
        "expected_value": "0.00",
        "diff_value": 0.00,
        "problem_count": 0,
        "detail_json": null,
        "is_overridden": false,
        "override_approval_no": null
      }
    ],
    "check_at": "2026-10-31T18:10:00",
    "elapsed_ms": 1240
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑（执行 12 项检查）：**

| 检查项 | 执行 SQL 要点 | 级别 |
|---|---|---|
| `VOUCHER_UNPOSTED` | `COUNT(*) WHERE period=? AND status IN ('SUBMITTED','AUDITED')` | `FAIL` |
| `VOUCHER_DRAFT` | `COUNT(*) WHERE period=? AND status='DRAFT'` | `WARN` |
| `VOUCHER_ENTRY_MISMATCH` | 明细汇总 vs 凭证头合计，比对行数 | `FAIL` |
| `TRIAL_BALANCE` | 三组借贷合计差额（复用 API-23） | `FAIL` |
| `BALANCE_DIRECTION_CHECK` | `COUNT(*) WHERE end_balance < 0` | `WARN` |
| `GENERAL_VS_DETAIL` | `COUNT(*) FROM m17_ledger_general WHERE is_balanced = FALSE` | `FAIL` |
| `LEDGER_VOUCHER_MATCH` | 明细账发生额 vs 凭证分录汇总（FULL JOIN 比对） | `FAIL` |
| `OPEN_BALANCE_CHECK` | 本期 `open_balance` vs 上期 `end_balance` | `FAIL` |
| `CASH_NEGATIVE` | `1001`/`1002` 的 `end_balance < 0` | `FAIL` |
| `PREVIOUS_PERIOD_CLOSED` | 上期 `status IN ('CLOSED','LOCKED')` | `FAIL` |
| `BANK_UNRECONCILED` | M17-06 未达账项数（外部查询，降级为 `WARN` + 提示） | `WARN` |
| `DEPT_PROFIT_CHECK` | 科室损益合计 vs 总账损益发生额（M17-04 对比） | `WARN` |

1) 逐项执行，结果落 `m17_period_close_check`（`period_close_id` 在结账时才回填，预检时可为 0 并随后更新）；2) `FAIL` 项任一失败 → `can_close = false` 并填 `block_reasons`；3) `WARN` 项 → `warn_count > 0`，`can_close` 仍为 `true` 但要求豁免审批；4) 结果缓存 Redis `m17:ledger:close_precheck:{period}` TTL 60s。

**错误码：** `"1705003"`、`"179001"`、`"1705038"`。

#### API-25 执行月末结账

```json
  "skip_warn_checks": ["VOUCHER_DRAFT", "BALANCE_DIRECTION_CHECK"],
  "override_approval_no": "AP202610310012"
}
```

**响应（成功）**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",

    "period_no": "PE202610",
    "status": "CLOSED",
    "old_period_status": "OPEN",
    "new_period_status": "CLOSED",
    "closing_voucher_no": "PV202610",
    "closing_voucher_id": 88100,
    "total_debit": 8620450.00,
    "total_credit": 8620450.00,
    "voucher_count": 5213,
    "next_period": "2026-11",
    "balance_carried_count": 1842,
    "closed_at": "2026-10-31T18:15:30",
    "closed_by_name": "张会计",
    "m16_notified": true,
    "m16_notified_at": "2026-10-31T18:15:32",
    "m16_event_id": "evt_8a2b1c3d4e5f6789",
    "m16_sync_status": "SYNCED",
    "elapsed_ms": 4120,
    "steps": [
      {"step": 1, "name": "行锁会计期间", "status": "SUCCESS", "elapsed_ms": 20},
      {"step": 2, "name": "校验前置条件", "status": "SUCCESS", "elapsed_ms": 380},
      {"step": 3, "name": "生成损益结转凭证", "status": "SUCCESS", "elapsed_ms": 1200},
      {"step": 4, "name": "结转余额至下期", "status": "SUCCESS", "elapsed_ms": 2450},
      {"step": 5, "name": "更新期间状态", "status": "SUCCESS", "elapsed_ms": 50},
      {"step": 6, "name": "发出 M16 锁账事件", "status": "SUCCESS", "elapsed_ms": 20}
    ]
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**响应（M16 回调失败）**

```json
{
  "code": "1705024",
  "message": "结账本地事务已完成，但 M16 锁账回调失败，期间停留在结账中，将每 5 分钟自动重试",
  "data": {
    "period": "2026-10",
    "status": "CLOSING",
    "closing_voucher_no": "PV202610",
    "m16_notified": false,
    "m16_notify_attempts": 1,
    "m16_notify_error": "connect timeout to m16-09-daily service",
    "next_retry_at": "2026-10-31T18:20:32",
    "alert_sent": true,
    "note": "在 M16 确认锁账前，本期不会被标记为已结账"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑（7 步，核心）：**

1. **行锁会计期间**：`SELECT * FROM m17_period_close WHERE period=? AND org_id=? FOR UPDATE`，防并发结账（`"1705030"`）。
2. **校验前置条件**：期间 `OPEN`（`"1705003"`）；试算平衡（`"1705012"`）；无 FAIL 级检查项（`"1705013"`）；无未过账凭证（`"1705014"`）；上期已结账（`"1705020"`）；**二次确认 `confirm = true`**（`"1705025"`）；有 WARN 豁免时必须有 `override_approval_no`（`"1705025"`）。
3. **生成损益结转凭证**：按 3.4（4）模型，把全部 `PL_INCOME`/`PL_EXPENSE` 科目期末余额转入 `3103 本年利润`；`voucher_no = PV{yyyyMM}`，`voucher_source = 'CLOSING'`，`is_closing = TRUE`；**该凭证本身也走借贷平衡 CHECK**（`"1705036"` 若不平衡）。
4. **结转余额至下期**：本期 `m17_ledger_balance` 全部置 `is_carry_forward = TRUE`、`carry_forward_to = next_period`；为该期间插入下期余额行，`open_balance = 本期 end_balance`。
5. **更新期间状态**：`m17_period_close.status = 'CLOSED'`、`closed_at`、`closed_by`、`total_debit` / `total_credit`（**必须相等，否则 `ck_m17_period_close_balance` 拒绝**）、`closing_voucher_no`、`trial_balanced = TRUE`、`check_passed = TRUE`；M17-01 期间表同步置 `CLOSED`。
6. **发 M16 锁账事件**：事务内写 outbox 表（`event_type = 'm17.period.closed'`，载荷含 `period`、`status`、`total_debit`、`total_credit`）；事务提交后由 Celery 投递。**投递成功后**才把 `m16_notified = TRUE`、`m16_notified_at`、`m16_event_id` 写回。若投递失败，`status` 保持 `CLOSING`（由 `ck_m17_period_close_m16_notified` 强制——不可能出现「已结账但未通知」）。
7. **收尾**：写审计日志 `CLOSE`（`is_sensitive = TRUE`、`is_financial_impact = TRUE`）；发事件 `m17.period.closed` 给 M22；通知财务主管（M03-05）；清余额与试算缓存。

> **为什么第 6 步要放在事务外？** 若在第 5 步（置 `CLOSED`）之前调用 M16，一旦 M16 失败，结账事务就要回滚——但此时本地的余额结转、凭证生成已做完，回滚代价大且可能留下部分状态。若在事务内调 M16，结账事务会长时间持有期间行锁，M16 的超时会导致结账超时。因此采用 **outbox 模式**：本地事务快速提交（`CLOSING`），投递异步进行，成功后才升级为 `CLOSED`。这保证了「快」与「必达」的平衡。

**错误码：** `"1705003"`、`"1705012"`、`"1705013"`、`"1705014"`、`"1705020"`、`"1705024"`、`"1705025"`、`"1705030"`、`"1705036"`、`"1705037"`、`"179001"`。

#### API-27 反结账

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m17/ledgers/close/2026-10/reopen
```

```json
{
  "period": "2026-10",
  "reason": "发现一笔 T+0 收入凭证漏记，需补录后重新结账",
  "approval_no": "AP202611050001",
  "confirm": true
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "period": "2026-10",
    "old_status": "CLOSED",
    "new_status": "REOPENED",
    "old_period_status": "CLOSED",
    "new_period_status": "OPEN",
    "reopen_count": 1,
    "reopen_voucher_no": "RV202610-0003",
    "balance_reverted": true,
    "m16_notified": true,
    "m16_event_id": "evt_7f8e9d0c1b2a3f4e",
    "reopened_at": "2026-11-05T10:20:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑（7 步）：**

1. 校验期间状态为 `CLOSED` 或 `LOCKED`（`"1705031"`）。
2. 校验后续期间未结账——若 `next_period` 及其后任一期已 `CLOSED`/`LOCKED`，必须先反结账后续期间（`"1705032"`，**倒序反结账，不可跳期**）。
3. 校验未超过 `AUTO_CLOSE_IMMUTABLE_DAYS`（默认 30 天，`"1705033"`）；超期需额外审批。
4. **必须有 `approval_no`**（`"1705022"`）与 `reason ≥5 字`（`"1705023"`）；调 M20 校验审批有效（降级为记录待补验）。
5. **生成红冲结账凭证**：把原结账凭证（损益结转）红冲（`RV` 前缀，`source = 'REVERSAL'`，`reverse_of_voucher_id` 指向 `PV202610`），并回冲下期期初余额。
6. 更新状态：`m17_period_close.status = 'REOPENED'`、`reopen_count + 1`、`reopened_at`、`reopen_reason`、`reopen_approval_no`、`reopen_voucher_no`；M17-01 期间表置 `OPEN`。
7. **发 `m17.period.reopened` 事件**通知 M16 重新开放该期日结（同样受 outbox + `m16_notified` 保障）；写审计日志 `REOPEN`（`is_sensitive = TRUE`，`approval_no` 必填）。

> **反结账的 `m16_notified` 语义：** 反结账后状态为 `REOPENED`（非 `CLOSING`），`ck_m17_period_close_m16_notified` 要求 `m16_notified = TRUE`。这里的语义是「M16 已与 M17 状态对齐」（即已收到重开通知），而非字面的「已通知关闭」。事件载荷中 `action` 字段区分 `CLOSE` / `REOPEN`，避免歧义。

**错误码：** `"1705031"`、`"1705032"`、`"1705033"`、`"1705022"`、`"1705023"`、`"1705024"`、`"1705010"`、`"179001"`。

```json
    "total_credit": 104286500.00,
```

```json
    "profit_expense_total": 15238400.00,
    "net_profit": 3188100.00,

    "undistributed_voucher_no": "V202612-0522",
```

1. 校验前置：12 期全部结账且 M16 已锁账（`"1705021"` / `"1705024"`）；`confirm = true`（`"1705025"`）。
2. **生成损益结转凭证**：把 12 月各损益类科目余额转入 `3103 本年利润`；**生成本年利润结转凭证**：`3103 本年利润` → `3104 利润分配-未分配利润`（按 3.4（5）模型，盈利与亏损方向相反）。
3. **生成次年余额**：为 2027-01 插入余额行——资产/负债/权益类 `open_balance = 2026-12 end_balance`；损益类 `open_balance = 0.00`。
4. **封存年度**：`m17_year_close.status = 'LOCKED'`、`locked_at`、`net_profit`（**CHECK 强制 = 收入 − 费用**）、全部结转凭证号、`next_year = fiscal_year + 1`；2026 全部 12 期状态置 `LOCKED`。写审计日志 `YEAR_CLOSE`；发事件 `m17.period.closed`（年度级）。

**错误码：** `"1705021"`、`"1705024"`、`"1705025"`、`"1705036"`、`"1705037"`、`"1705031"`、`"179001"`。

#### API-31 反年结

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m17/ledgers/year-close/2026/reopen
```

```json
{
  "fiscal_year": 2026,
  "reason": "外部审计提出 2026-11 有一笔跨期费用需调整，需重开年度账务",
  "approval_no": "AP20270315001",
  "confirm": true
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "fiscal_year": 2026,
    "old_status": "LOCKED",
    "new_status": "REOPENED",
    "revoked_voucher_nos": ["V202612-0521", "V202612-0522"],
    "periods_reopened": 0,
    "reopen_count": 1,
    "reopened_at": "2027-03-15T09:20:00",
    "next_step": "请对需要调整的期间执行反结账（倒序），修改完成后重新结账并年结"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验年度状态为 `LOCKED`（`"1705031"`）；2) **必须有院长级 `approval_no`**（`"1705022"`）与 `reason ≥10 字`（`"1705023"`）；调 M20 校验审批级别（降级为记录待补验）；3) `confirm = true`（`"1705025"`）；4) **红冲两张年结凭证**（损益结转 + 未分配利润结转），原凭证置 `REVERSED`；5) 年度状态置 `REOPENED`、`reopen_count + 1`；6) 12 期状态从 `LOCKED` 回退为 `CLOSED`（**注意：不是直接 `OPEN`**——反年结只是解除年度封存，要改某期还需再对该期执行反结账）；7) 写审计日志 `YEAR_REOPEN`（`is_sensitive = TRUE`，`approval_no` 必填）；8) 发事件通知 M22 年度报表需重出。

> **为什么反年结不直接把期间置为 `OPEN`？** 因为「解除年度封存」与「重新打开某一期」是两个不同粒度的操作，且后者需要单独审批。若反年结直接开放全部 12 期，等于用一次院长审批换来了全年可改，风险过大。分两步后，院长审批的是「解除封存」，而具体改哪一期由财务主管按需逐期审批，形成职责分离。

**错误码：** `"1705031"`、`"1705022"`、`"1705023"`、`"1705025"`、`"1705010"`、`"179001"`。

```json
    "period": "2026-10",
    "period_status": "CLOSED",
    "is_closed": true,
    "close_status": "CLOSED",
    "closed_at": "2026-10-31T18:15:30",
    "m16_notified": true,
    "m16_synced_at": "2026-10-31T18:15:32",
    "can_modify": false,
    "message": "2026-10 已结账并已同步 M16，本期日结不得修改",
    "query_time": "2026-11-05T10:20:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 按日期解析出会计期间；2) 查 `m17_period_close` 与 M17-01 期间状态；3) `can_modify = (period_status = 'OPEN')`——**M16-09 据此决定是否允许修改日结**；4) 结果缓存 Redis `m17:ledger:period:{period}` TTL 30s（M16 高频调用）；5) **SLA：P95 ≤ 20ms**（与共享规范 P-03 要求一致）。

> **这是本模块与 M16 之间最重要的契约。** M16-09 每次修改日结前都会调用；若本接口返回 `can_modify = false` 而 M16 仍允许修改，就是严重财务事故。因此本接口的响应必须与 `m17_period_close.status` 严格一致，且 `m16_notified` 状态同步对外暴露。

**错误码：** `40400`（该日期无对应期间）。

#### API-33 凭证查询（内部契约，供 M22）

**请求**

```
GET /internal/v1/m17/ledgers/vouchers/V202610-0001?period=2026-10
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "voucher_no": "V202610-0001",
    "period": "2026-10",
    "voucher_date": "2026-10-01",
    "voucher_source": "REVENUE",
    "summary": "2026-10-01 门诊日结收入",
    "debit_total": 129250.00,
    "credit_total": 129250.00,
    "entry_count": 5,
    "status": "POSTED",
    "is_balanced": true,
    "entries": [
      {"subject_code": "1001", "dc_flag": "D", "amount": 15300.00, "biz_line": "OUTPATIENT"},
      {"subject_code": "4001", "dc_flag": "C", "amount": 129250.00, "biz_line": "OUTPATIENT"}
    ],
    "query_time": "2026-11-05T10:20:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 供 M22 报表取数使用，返回凭证头与精简分录。

**错误码：** `40400`。

#### API-34 试算平衡结果查询（内部契约，供 M22）

**请求**

```
GET /internal/v1/m17/ledgers/trial-balance/2026-10?org_id=1
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "period": "2026-10",
    "round_no": 1,
    "is_balanced": true,
    "total_open_debit": 12480000.00,
    "total_open_credit": 12480000.00,
    "total_period_debit": 8620450.00,
    "total_period_credit": 8620450.00,
    "total_end_debit": 15320700.00,
    "total_end_credit": 15320700.00,
    "open_diff": 0.00,
    "period_diff": 0.00,
    "end_diff": 0.00,
    "subject_count": 1842,
    "check_at": "2026-10-31T18:02:15"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 查最新一轮试算平衡结果；供 M22 在出报表前校验账是否平衡（不平衡则报表标注「数据异常」）。

**错误码：** `40400`。

#### API-35 科目余额查询（内部契约 P-09）

**请求**

```
GET /internal/v1/m17/subjects/{code}/balance?period=2026-10&aux_type=DEPT&aux_code=D001&org_id=1
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "subject_code": "4001",
    "subject_name": "医疗收入",
    "period": "2026-10",
    "balance_direction": "CREDIT",
    "open_balance": 0.00,
    "period_debit": 0.00,
    "period_credit": 1286500.00,
    "end_balance": 1286500.00,
    "ytd_debit": 0.00,
    "ytd_credit": 8126400.00,
    "period_status": "CLOSED",
    "is_period_closed": true,
    "available_for_reporting": true,
    "query_time": "2026-11-05T10:20:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 按 `code` + `period`（+ 可选辅助核算）查 `m17_ledger_balance`，走 `uk_m17_ledger_balance_subject`；2) 若科目为非末级（有下级），实时汇总其下全部末级科目；3) 联查 `m17_period_close` 返回 `period_status` 与 `is_period_closed`——**M16/M22 可据此判断该余额是否可用于正式报表**；4) `available_for_reporting = is_period_closed`（未结账期间的余额仅供内部核对，不可作为正式报表口径）；5) `aux_type` 缺省为 `NONE`（无辅助核算的汇总余额）；6) 结果缓存 Redis `m17:ledger:subject_balance:{code}:{period}:{aux}` TTL 120s，结账/过账时主动失效。

**契约要点（与共享规范 §8 一致）：**

| 要点 | 值 |
|---|---|
| 路径 | `GET /internal/v1/m17/subjects/{code}/balance` **一字不差** |
| 调用方 | M16、M22 |
| SLA | **P95 ≤ 100ms** |
| 关键返回 | `end_balance`（期末余额）、`period_status`（期间状态）、`is_period_closed` |
| 不返回 | 任何敏感操作信息、任何可写入口 |

**错误码：** `"1705013"`（科目不存在）、`"1705012"`（期间不存在）。

#### API-36 生成结账凭证（内部契约，供 M17-01）

**请求**

```
POST /internal/v1/m17/ledgers/closing-voucher
Idempotency-Key: 8c2f5a1e-4b7d-9e3a-6c1f-2d8b5e9a3c7f
```

```json
{
  "period": "2026-10",
  "caller": "M17-01",
  "voucher_date": "2026-10-31",
  "carry_forward": true,
  "reversal_of_carry": false,
  "operator_id": 9001
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "period": "2026-10",
    "closing_voucher_no": "PV202610",
    "closing_voucher_id": 88100,
    "status": "POSTED",
    "profit_income_total": 1629300.00,
    "profit_expense_total": 1078000.00,
    "net_profit": 551300.00,
    "debit_total": 2707300.00,
    "credit_total": 2707300.00,
    "is_balanced": true,
    "entry_count": 6,
    "carry_forward_done": true,
    "carried_subject_count": 1842,
    "next_period": "2026-11",
    "idempotent": false,
    "elapsed_ms": 1180
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验 `period` 存在且状态为 `OPEN`/`REOPENED`（`"1705003"`）；2) **校验本期无未过账凭证**（复用 API-37，`"1705014"`）；3) 按 3.4（4）损益结转模型计算：`profit_income_total` = 全部 `PL_INCOME` 科目期末余额之和，`profit_expense_total` = 全部 `PL_EXPENSE` 科目期末余额之和，`net_profit = 收入 − 费用`；4) 生成结账凭证 `PV{yyyyMM}`：`voucher_source = 'CLOSING'`、`is_closing = TRUE`、凭证字 `转`，把损益类科目余额转入 `3103 本年利润`（**该凭证本身受 `ck_m17_voucher_balance` 约束**，不平衡则 `"1705036"`）；5) 用户可直接提交整批过账（`status = 'POSTED'`），使结账凭证立即生效；6) `carry_forward = true` 时把本期余额结转至 `next_period`（`"1705037"` 若结转失败）；7) `reversal_of_carry = true` 时执行反向结转（供反结账使用）；8) 该接口**只生成结账凭证与结转余额，不改期间状态、不发 M16 事件**——期间状态推进与 M16 锁账由 API-25（执行月末结账）负责，M17-01 在拿到本接口成功结果后调用 API-25 完成结账闭环；9) 同一期间重复调用返回既有结账凭证，`idempotent = true`；10) 支持 `Idempotency-Key`（Redis `m17:idem:ledger:closing_voucher:{key}` TTL 24h）。

**契约要点（M17-01 §10.1 U-13）：**

| 要点 | 值 |
|---|---|
| 路径 | `POST /internal/v1/m17/ledgers/closing-voucher` **一字不差** |
| 调用方 | M17-01 会计基础与科目体系（期间结账流程） |
| SLA | **P95 ≤ 500ms** |
| 失败影响 | **阻断**——期间结账必需，无结账凭证不得结账 |
| 幂等 | 同一期间重复调用返回既有 `PV{yyyyMM}` |
| 边界 | 只生成凭证与结转余额；**不改期间状态、不发 M16 事件**（后者由 API-25 负责） |

**错误码：** `"1705003"`、`"1705014"`、`"1705036"`、`"1705037"`、`"1705012"`、`"1705001"`。

#### API-37 未过账/未审核凭证数（内部契约，供 M17-01）

**请求**

```
POST /internal/v1/m17/ledgers/status-count
```

```json
{
  "period": "2026-10",
  "org_id": 1,
  "caller": "M17-01",
  "include_reversed": false
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "period": "2026-10",
    "total_count": 5213,
    "draft_count": 2,
    "submitted_count": 37,
    "audited_count": 124,
    "posted_count": 5052,
    "reversed_count": 124,
    "cancelled_count": 37,
    "unsubmitted_count": 39,
    "unposted_count": 161,
    "can_close": false,
    "block_reasons": [
      "存在 37 张待审核凭证",
      "存在 124 张已审核未过账凭证"
    ],
    "query_time": "2026-10-31T18:50:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 按 `period` + `org_id` 分组统计各状态凭证数（走 `idx_m17_voucher_period_status`，分区裁剪到单分区）；2) `unsubmitted_count = draft_count + submitted_count`（尚未走到审核完成的凭证数）；3) `unposted_count = submitted_count + audited_count`（**M17-01 结账校验的核心指标——必须为 0 才可结账**）；4) `can_close = (unposted_count = 0)`；5) `block_reasons` 逐条列出阻断原因，供 M17-01 直接展示或记录；6) `include_reversed = true` 时 `posted_count` 包含 `REVERSED`；7) 结果缓存 Redis `m17:ledger:status_count:{org}:{period}` TTL 30s（M17-01 结账时高频调用）；8) **SLA：P95 ≤ 100ms**。

**契约要点（M17-01 §10.1 U-14）：**

| 要点 | 值 |
|---|---|
| 路径 | `POST /internal/v1/m17/ledgers/status-count` **一字不差** |
| 调用方 | M17-01 会计基础与科目体系（结账前置校验） |
| SLA | **P95 ≤ 100ms** |
| 失败影响 | **阻断**——结账检查必需 |
| 关键字段 | `unposted_count`（必须为 0）、`can_close`、`block_reasons` |
| 只读 | 纯查询，不产生任何写操作 |

**错误码：** `40001`（`period` 格式错误）、`40400`（期间不存在）。

### 6.4 FastAPI 路由实现示例

```python
# app/api/v1/m17/ledger.py
"""M17-05 总账与期末结账 —— 路由层"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date
from typing import List, Optional

from fastapi import APIRouter, Body, Depends, Path, Query, Request
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.database import get_db
from app.core.response import PageResult, success_response
from app.core.security import get_current_user, require_permission
from app.schemas.m17.ledger import (
    BalanceQuery, LedgerQuery, PeriodCloseIn, PeriodReopenIn, VoucherAuditIn,
    VoucherCreateIn, VoucherPostIn, VoucherQuery, VoucherReversalIn,
    VoucherUpdateIn, YearCloseIn, YearReopenIn,
)
from app.services.m17.ledger_service import LedgerService
from app.services.m17.voucher_service import VoucherService
from app.services.m17.close_service import CloseService

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1/m17/ledgers", tags=["M17-05 总账与期末结账"])
internal_router = APIRouter(prefix="/internal/v1/m17",
                            tags=["M17-05 内部契约"])


# ==================== 工作台 ====================
@router.get("/workbench", summary="总账工作台概览")
async def workbench(
    period: str = Query(..., min_length=7, max_length=7, description="会计期间"),
    org_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, gt=0),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m17:ledger:view")),
):
    """返回本期概览、四项会计恒等式检查结果与结账状态。"""
    svc = LedgerService(db)
    data = await svc.workbench(period, org_id, user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.get("/workbench/todo", summary="待办事务明细")
async def workbench_todo(
    period: str = Query(..., min_length=7, max_length=7),
    org_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, gt=0),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m17:ledger:view")),
):
    """待审核、待过账、结账预检三类待办。"""
    svc = LedgerService(db)
    data = await svc.workbench_todo(period, org_id, user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.get("/workbench/period-progress", summary="近 6 期结账进度")
async def period_progress(
    org_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, gt=0),
    limit: int = Query(6, ge=1, le=24),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m17:ledger:view")),
):
    """展示近 N 期的试算、结账与 M16 锁账状态。"""
    svc = LedgerService(db)
    data = await svc.period_progress(org_id, limit, user)
    return success_response(data)


# ==================== 凭证 ====================
@router.get("/vouchers", summary="凭证分页列表")
async def list_vouchers(
    period: Optional[str] = Query(None, min_length=7, max_length=7),
    status: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="逗号分隔多选"),
    voucher_source: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    pending_audit: bool = Query(False),
    pending_post: bool = Query(False),
    keyword: Optional[str] = Query(None, max_length=64),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=200),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m17:ledger:view")),
):
    """多条件检索凭证，附带当前筛选结果的借贷合计与平衡标识。"""
    q = VoucherQuery(
        period=period,
        status=status.split(",") if status else None,
        voucher_source=voucher_source.split(",") if voucher_source else None,
        pending_audit=pending_audit,
        pending_post=pending_post,
        keyword=keyword,
        page=page,
        page_size=page_size,
    )
    svc = VoucherService(db)
    items, total, summary = await svc.list_vouchers(q, user)
    return success_response(PageResult(
        items=items, total=total, page=page, page_size=page_size,
        pages=(total + page_size - 1) // page_size,
        extra={"page_summary": summary}))


@router.get("/vouchers/posting-precheck", summary="过账前置校验")
async def posting_precheck(
    period: str = Query(..., min_length=7, max_length=7),
    pending_only: bool = Query(True),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m17:ledger:post")),
):
    """5 项前置校验：期间状态、明细合计、借贷平衡、末级科目、期间连续性。"""
    svc = VoucherService(db)
    data = await svc.posting_precheck(period, pending_only, user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.get("/vouchers/batch-posting-status/{batch_no}", summary="批量过账进度")
async def batch_posting_status(
    batch_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m17:ledger:post")),
):
    """读取异步过账任务的实时进度。"""
    svc = VoucherService(db)
    data = await svc.batch_posting_status(batch_no, user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.get("/vouchers/{voucher_no}", summary="凭证详情")
async def get_voucher(
    voucher_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    period: str = Query(..., min_length=7, max_length=7),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m17:ledger:view")),
):
    """返回凭证头、全部分录与四个可用操作布尔。"""
    svc = VoucherService(db)
    data = await svc.get_detail(voucher_no, period, user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.get("/vouchers/{voucher_no}/reversal-chain", summary="红冲链路追溯")
async def reversal_chain(
    voucher_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    period: str = Query(..., min_length=7, max_length=7),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m17:ledger:view")),
):
    """沿 reverse_of_voucher_id / reversed_by_voucher_id 双向追踪。"""
    svc = VoucherService(db)
    data = await svc.reversal_chain(voucher_no, period, user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.get("/vouchers/{voucher_no}/entries", summary="凭证明细分录")
async def get_entries(
    voucher_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    period: str = Query(..., min_length=7, max_length=7),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m17:ledger:view")),
):
    """返回分录与借贷合计平衡标识。"""
    svc = VoucherService(db)
    data = await svc.get_entries(voucher_no, period, user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.get("/vouchers/{voucher_no}/print", summary="凭证打印")
async def print_voucher(
    voucher_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    period: str = Query(..., min_length=7, max_length=7),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m17:ledger:export")),
):
    """生成标准记账凭证 PDF。"""
    svc = VoucherService(db)
    data = await svc.print_voucher(voucher_no, period, user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.post("/vouchers", summary="新建凭证")
async def create_voucher(
    payload: VoucherCreateIn,
    request: Request,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m17:ledger:create")),
):
    """校验期间 OPEN + 借贷平衡 + 末级科目后生成 V 号草稿；支持 Idempotency-Key。"""
    svc = VoucherService(db)
    data = await svc.create_voucher(
        payload, operator=user,
        idem_key=request.headers.get("Idempotency-Key"))
    return success_response(data)


@router.put("/vouchers/{voucher_no}", summary="更新凭证（仅 DRAFT）")
async def update_voucher(
    voucher_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    payload: VoucherUpdateIn = Body(...),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m17:ledger:create")),
):
    """仅草稿可改；业务凭证不可手工修改。"""
    svc = VoucherService(db)
    data = await svc.update_voucher(voucher_no, payload, operator=user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.post("/vouchers/{voucher_no}/submit", summary="提交凭证")
async def submit_voucher(
    voucher_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    version: int = Body(..., embed=True, ge=0),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m17:ledger:create")),
):
    """DRAFT → SUBMITTED，提交前复核借贷平衡。"""
    svc = VoucherService(db)
    data = await svc.submit_voucher(voucher_no, version, operator=user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.post("/vouchers/audit", summary="凭证审核")
async def audit_vouchers(
    payload: VoucherAuditIn = Body(...),
    voucher_nos: List[str] = Body(..., embed=True),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m17:ledger:audit")),
):
    """审核人不得等于制单人；驳回必须填原因。"""
    svc = VoucherService(db)
    data = await svc.audit_vouchers(voucher_nos, payload, operator=user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.post("/vouchers/post", summary="凭证过账")
async def post_vouchers(
```
