
### 6.5 Service 层核心逻辑示例

```python
# app/services/m17/voucher_posting_service.py
"""M17-05 凭证过账服务 —— 账簿生成的核心（本子模块最关键的服务）"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

from sqlalchemy import and_, func, select, text, update
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.exceptions import BizException
from app.core.redis import redis_client
from app.models.m17.balance import M17LedgerBalance, M17LedgerGeneral
from app.models.m17.ledger import M17LedgerDetail
from app.models.m17.period import M17LedgerLog
from app.models.m17.voucher import M17Voucher, M17VoucherEntry

ZERO = Decimal("0.00")


class VoucherPostingService:
    """凭证过账服务：把凭证写进账簿。

    会计铁律在过账中的体现：
      ① 过账前校验明细合计 = 凭证头合计（"1705002"）；
      ② 生成余额行时按借贷方向计算期末余额，
         算错会被 ck_m17_ledger_balance_debit / _credit 拒绝；
      ③ 过账后凭证 is_locked = TRUE，被触发器保护不可再改（"1705005"）。
    """

    def __init__(self, db: AsyncSession) -> None:
        """初始化。"""
        self.db = db

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 过账前置校验
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    async def precheck(self, period: str,
                       pending_only: bool = True) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """执行 5 项过账前置校验。"""
        checks: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

        # ① 期间状态必须 OPEN
        period_status = (await self.db.execute(
            text(
                "SELECT status FROM m17_period_close "
                "WHERE period = :p AND org_id = 1"
            ),
            {"p": period},
        )).scalar()
        # 无结账记录视为期间未结账（OPEN）
        is_open = period_status in (None, "REOPENED")
        checks.append({
            "check_code": "PERIOD_STATUS",
            "check_name": "期间状态",
            "passed": is_open,
            "detail": "OPEN" if is_open else str(period_status),
        })

        # ② 明细合计 = 凭证头合计
        mismatch_rows = (await self.db.execute(
            text(
                """
                SELECT count(*) FROM m17_voucher v
                JOIN (
                    SELECT voucher_id, period,
                           SUM(CASE WHEN dc_flag = 'D' THEN amount ELSE 0 END) dr,
                           SUM(CASE WHEN dc_flag = 'C' THEN amount ELSE 0 END) cr
                      FROM m17_voucher_entry
                     WHERE period = :p
                     GROUP BY voucher_id, period
                ) e ON e.voucher_id = v.id AND e.period = v.period
                WHERE v.period = :p
                  AND (:pending_only = FALSE OR v.status = 'AUDITED')
                  AND (e.dr <> v.debit_total OR e.cr <> v.credit_total)
                """
            ),
            {"p": period, "pending_only": pending_only},
        )).scalar() or 0
        checks.append({
            "check_code": "ENTRY_TOTAL",
            "check_name": "明细合计校验",
            "passed": mismatch_rows == 0,
            "detail": ("%d 张凭证明细合计与凭证头不一致" % mismatch_rows
                       if mismatch_rows else "明细合计与凭证头一致"),
        })

        # ③ 借贷平衡
        totals = (await self.db.execute(
            text(
                "SELECT COALESCE(SUM(debit_total), 0) dr, "
                "COALESCE(SUM(credit_total), 0) cr "
                "FROM m17_voucher "
                "WHERE period = :p AND status = 'AUDITED'"
            ),
            {"p": period},
        )).first()
        dr = Decimal(str(totals.dr or 0)).quantize(ZERO)
        cr = Decimal(str(totals.cr or 0)).quantize(ZERO)
        checks.append({
            "check_code": "VOUCHER_BALANCE",
            "check_name": "借贷平衡校验",
            "passed": dr == cr,
            "detail": "借方 %s = 贷方 %s" % (dr, cr) if dr == cr
                      else "借方 %s ≠ 贷方 %s" % (dr, cr),
        })

        # ④ 末级科目校验（科目表标记 is_leaf）
        non_leaf = (await self.db.execute(
            text(
                """
                SELECT count(*) FROM m17_voucher_entry e
                JOIN m17_account_subject s ON s.code = e.subject_code
                WHERE e.period = :p AND s.is_leaf = FALSE
                """
            ),
            {"p": period},
        )).scalar() or 0
        checks.append({
            "check_code": "LEAF_SUBJECT",
            "check_name": "末级科目校验",
            "passed": non_leaf == 0,
            "detail": ("存在 %d 条非末级科目分录" % non_leaf if non_leaf
                       else "全部为末级科目"),
        })

        # ⑤ 期间连续性：上期必须已结账
        prev = self._prev_period(period)
        prev_closed = (await self.db.execute(
            text(
                "SELECT status FROM m17_period_close "
                "WHERE period = :p AND org_id = 1"
            ),
            {"p": prev},
        )).scalar()
        checks.append({
            "check_code": "PREVIOUS_PERIOD",
            "check_name": "期间连续性",
            "passed": prev_closed in ("CLOSED", "LOCKED"),
            "detail": "%s %s" % (prev, prev_closed or "未结账"),
        })

        pending = (await self.db.execute(
            text("SELECT count(*) FROM m17_voucher "
                 "WHERE period = :p AND status = 'AUDITED'"),
            {"p": period},
        )).scalar() or 0

        return {
            "period": period,
            "can_post": all(c["passed"] for c in checks),
            "checks": checks,
            "pending_count": pending,
            "pending_debit_total": float(dr),
            "pending_credit_total": float(cr),
        }

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 过账主流程
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    async def post(self, period: str, voucher_nos: List[str],
                   operator: Any, check_balance: bool = True,
                   continue_on_error: bool = False) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """执行过账：校验 → 生成明细账 → 更新余额 → 更新总账 → 置状态。"""
        started = datetime.now()

        # ---------- 步骤 1：期间与连续性校验 ----------
        await self._assert_period_open(period)
        await self._assert_previous_closed(period)

        # ---------- 步骤 2：取凭证并逐张校验 ----------
        vouchers = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M17Voucher).where(
                M17Voucher.period == period,
                M17Voucher.voucher_no.in_(voucher_nos),
            ).order_by(M17Voucher.voucher_no)
        )).scalars().all()
        if len(vouchers) != len(voucher_nos):
            raise BizException("40400", "部分凭证不存在")

        for v in vouchers:
            if v.status != "AUDITED":
                raise BizException(
                    "1705004",
                    "凭证 %s 状态为 %s，不允许过账" % (v.voucher_no, v.status))
            # 【职责分离】过账人不得等于制单人
            if v.maker_id is not None and v.maker_id == getattr(operator, "id", None):
                raise BizException(
                    "1705010",
                    "违反职责分离：过账人不能是凭证 %s 的制单人" % v.voucher_no)

        # ---------- 步骤 3：明细合计校验 ----------
        if check_balance:
            await self._assert_entry_total(period, vouchers)

        # ---------- 步骤 4：末级科目校验 ----------
        await self._assert_leaf_subject(period, vouchers)

        # ---------- 步骤 5：生成明细账 ----------
        detail_rows = await self._build_ledger_details(period, vouchers,
                                                       operator)

        # ---------- 步骤 6：更新余额表（借贷方向不同公式） ----------
        await self._upsert_balances(period, detail_rows)

        # ---------- 步骤 7：更新总账 ----------
        await self._upsert_general(period)

        # ---------- 步骤 8：置凭证状态（含锁定） ----------
        now = datetime.now()
        total_dr = sum((Decimal(str(v.debit_total)) for v in vouchers), ZERO)
        total_cr = sum((Decimal(str(v.credit_total)) for v in vouchers), ZERO)
        await self.db.execute(
            update(M17Voucher)
            .where(M17Voucher.period == period,
                   M17Voucher.voucher_no.in_(voucher_nos))
            .values(status="POSTED", is_locked=True,
                    poster_id=getattr(operator, "id", None),
                    poster_name=getattr(operator, "name", None),
                    posted_at=now,
                    period_status_snap="OPEN")
        )

        await self._write_log(period, "POST", operator,
                              debit_total=total_dr, credit_total=total_cr)
        await self.db.commit()

        # ---------- 步骤 9：清缓存 + 发事件 ----------
        await self._invalidate_cache(period)

        elapsed = int((datetime.now() - started).total_seconds() * 1000)
        return {
            "success_count": len(vouchers),
            "failed_count": 0,
            "elapsed_ms": elapsed,
            "debit_total": float(total_dr),
            "credit_total": float(total_cr),
            "ledger_detail_count": len(detail_rows),
            "posted_at": now,
        }

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 明细账生成
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    async def _build_ledger_details(
        self, period: str, vouchers: List[M17Voucher], operator: Any,
    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """把凭证分录展开为明细账行，并计算逐笔余额。"""
        voucher_ids = [v.id for v in vouchers]
        entries = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M17VoucherEntry).where(
                M17VoucherEntry.period == period,
                M17VoucherEntry.voucher_id.in_(voucher_ids),
            ).order_by(M17VoucherEntry.voucher_id, M17VoucherEntry.entry_no)
        )).scalars().all()

        # 取本期已有的各科目期末余额作为逐笔余额的起点
        running: Dict[Tuple[str, str, str], Decimal] = {}
        balance_rows = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M17LedgerBalance).where(M17LedgerBalance.period == period)
        )).scalars().all()
        for b in balance_rows:
            running[(b.subject_code, b.aux_type, b.aux_code)] = Decimal(
                str(b.end_balance))

        # 科目方向映射（决定 running_balance 的加减方向）
        directions = await self._subject_directions([e.subject_code for e in entries])

        voucher_map = {v.id: v for v in vouchers}
        posted_at = datetime.now()
        rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

        for e in entries:
            v = voucher_map[e.voucher_id]
            # 对方科目：同凭证中方向相反的首个科目
            counter = next(
                (x.subject_code for x in entries
                 if x.voucher_id == e.voucher_id and x.dc_flag != e.dc_flag),
                None,
            )
            key = (e.subject_code, e.aux_type or "NONE", e.aux_code or "NONE")
            prev = running.get(key, ZERO)
            direction = directions.get(e.subject_code, "DEBIT")
            amount = Decimal(str(e.amount))

            # 逐笔余额：借方科目 到期初+借-贷；贷方科目 到期初+贷-借
            if direction == "DEBIT":
                delta = amount if e.dc_flag == "D" else -amount
            else:
                delta = amount if e.dc_flag == "C" else -amount
            new_balance = (prev + delta).quantize(ZERO)
            running[key] = new_balance

            row = M17LedgerDetail(
                period=period,
                voucher_id=e.voucher_id,
                voucher_no=v.voucher_no,
                entry_id=e.id,
                entry_no=e.entry_no,
                voucher_date=v.voucher_date,
                voucher_word=v.voucher_word,
                subject_code=e.subject_code,
                subject_name=e.subject_name,
                subject_category=e.subject_category,
                dc_flag=e.dc_flag,
                debit_amount=amount if e.dc_flag == "D" else ZERO,
                credit_amount=amount if e.dc_flag == "C" else ZERO,
                balance_direction=direction,
                running_balance=new_balance,
                aux_type=e.aux_type,
                aux_code=e.aux_code,
                aux_name=e.aux_name,
                dept_id=e.dept_id,
                biz_line=e.biz_line,
                summary=e.entry_summary or v.summary,
                counter_subject=counter,
                is_reversal=v.is_reversal,
                posted_at=posted_at,
                org_id=v.org_id,
            )
            self.db.add(row)
            rows.append({
                "subject_code": e.subject_code,
                "aux_type": e.aux_type or "NONE",
                "aux_code": e.aux_code or "NONE",
                "dc_flag": e.dc_flag,
                "amount": amount,
                "direction": direction,
                "subject_name": e.subject_name,
                "subject_category": e.subject_category,
                "dept_id": e.dept_id,
            })
        await self.db.flush()
        return rows

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 余额表更新（借贷两个方向分别计算）
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    async def _upsert_balances(self, period: str,
                               rows: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
        """按科目+辅助核算汇总，更新余额表。

        期末余额按余额方向计算，写错会被数据库 CHECK 拒绝：
          借方科目：end = open + period_debit - period_credit
          贷方科目：end = open + period_credit - period_debit
        """
        agg: Dict[Tuple[str, str, str], Dict[str, Any]] = {}
        for r in rows:
            key = (r["subject_code"], r["aux_type"], r["aux_code"])
            slot = agg.setdefault(key, {
                "dr": ZERO, "cr": ZERO, "direction": r["direction"],
                "name": r["subject_name"], "category": r["subject_category"],
                "dept_id": r["dept_id"],
            })
            if r["dc_flag"] == "D":
                slot["dr"] += r["amount"]
            else:
                slot["cr"] += r["amount"]

        for (code, aux_type, aux_code), val in agg.items():
            row = (await self.db.execute(
                select(M17LedgerBalance).where(
                    M17LedgerBalance.subject_code == code,
                    M17LedgerBalance.period == period,
                    M17LedgerBalance.aux_type == aux_type,
                    M17LedgerBalance.aux_code == aux_code,
                ).with_for_update()
            )).scalar_one_or_none()

            if row is None:
                # 新科目：期初取上期期末（若已结转），否则 0.00
                open_bal = await self._carried_open_balance(code, period,
                                                            aux_type, aux_code)
                row = M17LedgerBalance(
                    period=period, subject_code=code,
                    subject_name=val["name"],
                    subject_category=val["category"],
                    balance_direction=val["direction"],
                    aux_type=aux_type, aux_code=aux_code,
                    dept_id=val["dept_id"],
                    open_balance=open_bal,
                    period_debit=ZERO, period_credit=ZERO,
                    end_balance=open_bal,
                    ytd_debit=ZERO, ytd_credit=ZERO,
                )
                self.db.add(row)

            row.period_debit = (Decimal(str(row.period_debit)) + val["dr"]).quantize(ZERO)
            row.period_credit = (Decimal(str(row.period_credit)) + val["cr"]).quantize(ZERO)
            row.ytd_debit = (Decimal(str(row.ytd_debit)) + val["dr"]).quantize(ZERO)
            row.ytd_credit = (Decimal(str(row.ytd_credit)) + val["cr"]).quantize(ZERO)

            # ★ 按余额方向计算期末（写错方向会被 CHECK 拒绝）
            open_bal = Decimal(str(row.open_balance))
            if row.balance_direction == "DEBIT":
                row.end_balance = (open_bal + row.period_debit
                                   - row.period_credit).quantize(ZERO)
            else:
                row.end_balance = (open_bal + row.period_credit
                                   - row.period_debit).quantize(ZERO)
            row.entry_count = int(row.entry_count or 0) + 1
            row.version = int(row.version or 0) + 1
        await self.db.flush()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 总账更新
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    async def _upsert_general(self, period: str) -> None:
        """按一级科目汇总余额表，写入总账并核对总账与明细账。"""
        results = (await self.db.execute(
            text(
                """
                SELECT split_part(subject_code, '', 1) AS unused,
                       substring(subject_code FROM 1 FOR 4) AS top_code,
                       balance_direction,
                       SUM(open_balance)   AS open_sum,
                       SUM(period_debit)   AS dr_sum,
                       SUM(period_credit)  AS cr_sum,
                       SUM(end_balance)    AS end_sum,
                       SUM(ytd_debit)      AS ytd_dr,
                       SUM(ytd_credit)     AS ytd_cr,
                       COUNT(*)            AS child_cnt
                  FROM m17_ledger_balance
                 WHERE period = :p
                 GROUP BY substring(subject_code FROM 1 FOR 4), balance_direction
                """
            ),
            {"p": period},
        )).fetchall()

        for r in results:
            general = (await self.db.execute(
                select(M17LedgerGeneral).where(
                    M17LedgerGeneral.subject_code == r.top_code,
                    M17LedgerGeneral.period == period,
                )
            )).scalar_one_or_none()
            if general is None:
                general = M17LedgerGeneral(
                    period=period, subject_code=r.top_code,
                    subject_category="ASSET",
                    balance_direction=r.balance_direction,
                )
                self.db.add(general)

            open_sum = Decimal(str(r.open_sum or 0)).quantize(ZERO)
            dr_sum = Decimal(str(r.dr_sum or 0)).quantize(ZERO)
            cr_sum = Decimal(str(r.cr_sum or 0)).quantize(ZERO)
            general.open_balance = open_sum
            general.period_debit = dr_sum
            general.period_credit = cr_sum
            general.ytd_debit = Decimal(str(r.ytd_dr or 0)).quantize(ZERO)
            general.ytd_credit = Decimal(str(r.ytd_cr or 0)).quantize(ZERO)
            general.child_subject_count = int(r.child_cnt or 0)

            if general.balance_direction == "DEBIT":
                general.end_balance = (open_sum + dr_sum - cr_sum).quantize(ZERO)
            else:
                general.end_balance = (open_sum + cr_sum - dr_sum).quantize(ZERO)

            # 总账与明细账核对：两者必须相等，相等后才置 is_balanced
            general.leaf_balance_sum = Decimal(str(r.end_sum or 0)).quantize(ZERO)
            if general.end_balance == general.leaf_balance_sum:
                general.is_balanced = True
                general.balanced_at = datetime.now()
            else:
                general.is_balanced = False
        await self.db.flush()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 校验与工具
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    async def _assert_period_open(self, period: str) -> None:
        """期间必须 OPEN（无结账记录视为 OPEN）。"""
        status = (await self.db.execute(
            text("SELECT status FROM m17_period_close "
                 "WHERE period = :p AND org_id = 1"),
            {"p": period},
        )).scalar()
        if status in ("CLOSED", "LOCKED"):
            raise BizException(
                "1705003",
                "会计期间 %s 状态为 %s，不允许过账" % (period, status))

    async def _assert_previous_closed(self, period: str) -> None:
        """上期必须已结账，防止跳期过账。"""
        prev = self._prev_period(period)
        status = (await self.db.execute(
            text("SELECT status FROM m17_period_close "
                 "WHERE period = :p AND org_id = 1"),
            {"p": prev},
        )).scalar()
        if status not in ("CLOSED", "LOCKED"):
            # 首期（无历史结账记录）允许通过；否则阻断
            has_any = (await self.db.execute(
                text("SELECT count(*) FROM m17_voucher WHERE period = :p"),
                {"p": prev},
            )).scalar() or 0
            if has_any:
                raise BizException(
                    "1705020",
                    "上期 %s 未结账，不可过账本期" % prev)

    async def _assert_entry_total(self, period: str,
                                  vouchers: List[M17Voucher]) -> None:
        """明细合计必须等于凭证头合计。"""
        ids = [v.id for v in vouchers]
        bad = (await self.db.execute(
            text(
                """
                SELECT v.voucher_no, v.debit_total, v.credit_total, e.dr, e.cr
                  FROM m17_voucher v
                  JOIN (
                      SELECT voucher_id,
                             SUM(CASE WHEN dc_flag='D' THEN amount ELSE 0 END) dr,
                             SUM(CASE WHEN dc_flag='C' THEN amount ELSE 0 END) cr
                        FROM m17_voucher_entry
                       WHERE period = :p AND voucher_id = ANY(:ids)
                       GROUP BY voucher_id
                  ) e ON e.voucher_id = v.id
                 WHERE v.period = :p
                   AND (e.dr <> v.debit_total OR e.cr <> v.credit_total)
                """
            ),
            {"p": period, "ids": ids},
        )).fetchall()
        if bad:
            first = bad[0]
            raise BizException(
                "1705002",
                "凭证 %s 明细合计（借 %s / 贷 %s）与凭证头（借 %s / 贷 %s）不一致"
                % (first.voucher_no, first.dr, first.cr,
                   first.debit_total, first.credit_total))

    async def _assert_leaf_subject(self, period: str,
                                   vouchers: List[M17Voucher]) -> None:
        """全部分录科目必须为末级科目。"""
        ids = [v.id for v in vouchers]
        bad = (await self.db.execute(
            text(
                """
                SELECT DISTINCT e.subject_code
                  FROM m17_voucher_entry e
                  JOIN m17_account_subject s ON s.code = e.subject_code
                 WHERE e.period = :p AND e.voucher_id = ANY(:ids)
                   AND s.is_leaf = FALSE
                 LIMIT 5
                """
            ),
            {"p": period, "ids": ids},
        )).scalars().all()
        if bad:
            raise BizException(
                "1705011",
                "存在非末级科目不可记账：%s" % ", ".join(bad))

    async def _subject_directions(self, codes: List[str]) -> Dict[str, str]:
        """取科目余额方向（决定逐笔余额的加减方向）。"""
        rows = (await self.db.execute(
            text("SELECT code, balance_direction FROM m17_account_subject "
                 "WHERE code = ANY(:codes)"),
            {"codes": list(set(codes))},
        )).fetchall()
        return {r.code: r.balance_direction for r in rows}

    async def _carried_open_balance(self, code: str, period: str,
                                    aux_type: str,
                                    aux_code: str) -> Decimal:
        """取上期结转来的期初余额；无上期则为 0.00。"""
        prev = self._prev_period(period)
        val = (await self.db.execute(
            text(
                "SELECT b.end_balance FROM m17_ledger_balance b "
                "JOIN m17_period_close pc ON pc.period = b.period "
                "WHERE b.subject_code = :c AND b.period = :p "
                "  AND b.aux_type = :at AND b.aux_code = :ac "
                "  AND pc.is_balanced IS NOT NULL"
            ),
            {"c": code, "p": prev, "at": aux_type, "ac": aux_code},
        )).scalar()
        return Decimal(str(val)).quantize(ZERO) if val is not None else ZERO

    @staticmethod
    def _prev_period(period: str) -> str:
        """返回上一个会计期间。"""
        year, month = int(period[:4]), int(period[5:7])
        if month == 1:
            return "%04d-12" % (year - 1)
        return "%04d-%02d" % (year, month - 1)

    async def _write_log(self, period: str, action: str, operator: Any,
                         debit_total: Optional[Decimal] = None,
                         credit_total: Optional[Decimal] = None) -> None:
        """写审计日志（借贷快照必然平衡，受 ck_m17_ledger_log_balance 约束）。"""
        log_no = "LL%s%08d" % (
            datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d"),
            await redis_client.incr("m17:code:LL"))
        self.db.add(M17LedgerLog(
            log_no=log_no,
            action=action,
            action_group="VOUCHER",
            period=period,
            debit_total=debit_total,
            credit_total=credit_total,
            is_financial_impact=True,
            operator_id=getattr(operator, "id", None),
            operator_name=getattr(operator, "name", None),
            operator_role=getattr(operator, "role", None),
            result="SUCCESS",
            operated_at=datetime.now(),
        ))

    async def _invalidate_cache(self, period: str) -> None:
        """过账后失效余额、工作台、试算相关缓存。"""
        for pattern in ("m17:ledger:balance:*",
                        "m17:ledger:workbench:*",
                        "m17:ledger:subject_balance:*",
                        "m17:ledger:close_precheck:*"):
            async for key in redis_client.scan_iter(pattern):
                await redis_client.delete(key)
```

---

## 7. 缓存与异步任务设计

### 7.1 Redis 缓存设计

| Key 模式 | 类型 | TTL | 说明 | 失效时机 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `m17:ledger:workbench:{org}:{period}` | String(JSON) | 120s | 工作台概览（含恒等式检查） | 过账/结账/凭证状态变化 |
| `m17:ledger:balance:{org}:{period}:{filter}` | String(JSON) | 120s | 科目余额表查询结果 | 过账、结账、反结账 |
| `m17:ledger:subject_balance:{code}:{period}:{aux}` | String(JSON) | 120s | **P-09 科目余额** | 过账、结账 |
| `m17:ledger:general:{org}:{period}` | String(JSON) | 120s | 总账汇总 | 过账、结账 |
| `m17:ledger:close_precheck:{period}` | String(JSON) | 60s | 结账预检结果（12 项） | 执行预检时刷新、过账后失效 |
| `m17:ledger:trial:{period}:{round}` | String(JSON) | 300s | 试算平衡结果 | 新一轮试算、过账后失效 |
| `m17:ledger:period:{period}` | String(JSON) | 30s | **期间状态（M16-09 高频调用）** | 结账、反结账 |
| `m17:ledger:posting:{batch_no}` | Hash | 2h | 批量过账任务进度 | 任务完成后保留 2 小时 |
| `m17:ledger:detail:{period}:{code}` | String(JSON) | 120s | 明细账首页数据 | 过账 |
| `m17:ledger:close_lock:{period}` | String | 300s | **结账分布式锁** | 结账完成后释放 |
| `m17:idem:ledger:create:{key}` | String | 24h | 新建凭证幂等键 | 自然过期 |
| `m17:idem:ledger:post:{key}` | String | 24h | **过账幂等键** | 自然过期 |
| `m17:idem:ledger:close:{key}` | String | 24h | **结账幂等键** | 自然过期 |
| `m17:code:V:{yyyyMM}` | String(计数器) | 60d | 凭证号月度流水 | 自然过期 |
| `m17:code:RV:{yyyyMM}` | String(计数器) | 60d | 冲销凭证号流水 | 自然过期 |
| `m17:code:LL:{yyyyMMdd}` | String(计数器) | 48h | 审计日志号日流水 | 自然过期 |
| `m17:event:outbox:{event_id}` | String | 7d | 事件投递状态（幂等） | 自然过期 |

**容量估算：**

| 数据 | 单条大小 | 数量 | 内存 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 工作台概览 | 6 KB | 24（12 期 × 2 院区） | 0.14 MB |
| 科目余额表页 | 40 KB | 60 | 2.4 MB |
| P-09 科目余额 | 1.2 KB | 3,000（科目 × 期间） | 3.6 MB |
| 总账汇总 | 20 KB | 48 | 0.96 MB |
| 结账预检 | 12 KB | 24 | 0.29 MB |
| 试算结果 | 4 KB | 60 | 0.24 MB |
| 期间状态 | 0.4 KB | 60 | 0.02 MB |
| 过账进度 | 2 KB | 20 | 0.04 MB |
| 幂等键 | 0.8 KB | 2,000 | 1.6 MB |
| 计数器 | 0.1 KB | 100 | 0.01 MB |
| **合计** | — | — | **约 9.3 MB** |

> **设计说明：** 缓存体量很小（< 10 MB），因为核心表（余额表约 3.6 万行/年）本身查询就已很快。缓存的价值在于：①**P-09 的 100ms SLA**（M16/M22 高频调用）；②**期间状态的 20ms SLA**（M16-09 每次改日结前都要调）；③看板类页面的重复刷新。

### 7.2 Celery 异步任务

```python
# app/tasks/m17/ledger_tasks.py
"""M17-05 总账与期末结账 —— Celery 异步任务"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import logging
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from celery import shared_task

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


@shared_task(bind=True, max_retries=3, default_retry_delay=60)
def post_vouchers_async_task(self: Any, period: str, batch_no: str,
                             voucher_nos: List[str],
                             operator_id: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """大批量凭证过账（超过 200 张时由 API-12 转异步）。"""
    try:
        return asyncio.run(_post_vouchers(period, batch_no, voucher_nos,
                                          operator_id))
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        logger.warning("post_vouchers_async retry: %s", exc)
        raise self.retry(exc=exc, countdown=60 * (2 ** self.request.retries))


async def _post_vouchers(period: str, batch_no: str,
                         voucher_nos: List[str],
                         operator_id: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """分批过账并更新 Redis 进度。"""
    import json

    from app.core.database import AsyncSessionLocal
    from app.core.redis import redis_client
    from app.services.m17.voucher_posting_service import VoucherPostingService

    progress_key = "m17:ledger:posting:%s" % batch_no
    total = len(voucher_nos)
    processed = success = failed = 0
    failed_items: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    batch_size = 100

    for i in range(0, total, batch_size):
        chunk = voucher_nos[i:i + batch_size]
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
            svc = VoucherPostingService(db)
            try:
                await svc.post(period, chunk,
                               operator={"id": operator_id},
                               continue_on_error=False)
                success += len(chunk)
            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
                failed += len(chunk)
                failed_items.extend(
                    {"voucher_no": no, "reason": str(exc)[:200]} for no in chunk)
        processed += len(chunk)
        await redis_client.hset(progress_key, mapping={
            "status": "RUNNING",
            "total": total,
            "processed": processed,
            "success_count": success,
            "failed_count": failed,
            "progress_pct": round(processed * 100.0 / total, 2),
            "failed_items": json.dumps(failed_items, ensure_ascii=False),
        })
        await redis_client.expire(progress_key, 7200)

    await redis_client.hset(progress_key, mapping={
        "status": "COMPLETED",
        "finished_at": __import__("datetime").datetime.now().isoformat(),
    })
    return {"batch_no": batch_no, "total": total,
            "success": success, "failed": failed}


@shared_task(bind=True, max_retries=288, default_retry_delay=300)
def notify_m16_period_closed_task(self: Any, period: str,
                                  event_id: str,
                                  action: str = "CLOSE") -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """投递期间结账/重开事件给 M16（每 5 分钟重试，最多 24 小时）。

    会计铁律 5：结账后必须回调 M16 锁账。
    在 M16 确认前，期间保持 CLOSING 状态，不会被标记为已结账。
    """
    try:
        return asyncio.run(_notify_m16(period, event_id, action))
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        logger.warning("notify_m16_period retry #%d: %s",
                       self.request.retries, exc)
        asyncio.run(_record_notify_failure(period, str(exc)))
        raise self.retry(exc=exc, countdown=300)


async def _notify_m16(period: str, event_id: str,
                      action: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """投递 m17.period.closed 事件并回写 m16_notified。"""
    from datetime import datetime

    from sqlalchemy import update

    from app.clients.m17.m16_client import M16Client
    from app.core.database import AsyncSessionLocal
    from app.models.m17.period import M17PeriodClose

    m16 = M16Client()
    await m16.on_period_closed(period=period, event_id=event_id, action=action)

    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        await db.execute(
            update(M17PeriodClose)
            .where(M17PeriodClose.period == period)
            .values(m16_notified=True,
                    m16_notified_at=datetime.now(),
                    m16_event_id=event_id)
        )
        await db.commit()
    logger.info("m16 notified for period %s (action=%s)", period, action)
    return {"period": period, "m16_notified": True, "event_id": event_id}


async def _record_notify_failure(period: str, error: str) -> None:
    """记录回调失败原因与尝试次数（供页面展示与告警）。"""
    from sqlalchemy import text

    from app.core.database import AsyncSessionLocal

    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        await db.execute(
            text(
                "UPDATE m17_period_close "
                "SET m16_notify_attempts = m16_notify_attempts + 1, "
                "    m16_notify_error = :e, "
                "    updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP "
                "WHERE period = :p AND m16_notified = FALSE"
            ),
            {"p": period, "e": error[:500]},
        )
        await db.commit()


@shared_task
def export_ledger_detail_task(task_id: str, period: str,
                              subject_code: str,
                              operator_id: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """异步导出标准会计明细账 Excel。"""
    return asyncio.run(_export_detail(task_id, period, subject_code,
                                      operator_id))


async def _export_detail(task_id: str, period: str, subject_code: str,
                         operator_id: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """生成 Excel 并写入下载地址。"""
    from app.services.m17.ledger_export_service import LedgerExportService
    svc = LedgerExportService()
    return await svc.export_detail(task_id, period, subject_code, operator_id)


@shared_task
def export_vouchers_task(task_id: str, query: Dict[str, Any],
                         operator_id: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """异步导出凭证清单（含分录明细）。"""
    return asyncio.run(_export_vouchers(task_id, query, operator_id))


async def _export_vouchers(task_id: str, query: Dict[str, Any],
                           operator_id: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """按查询条件导出凭证 Excel。"""
    from app.services.m17.ledger_export_service import LedgerExportService
    svc = LedgerExportService()
    return await svc.export_vouchers(task_id, query, operator_id)


@shared_task
def notify_close_result_task(period: str, success: bool,
                             message: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """结账成功/失败通知财务主管（M03-05）。"""
    return asyncio.run(_notify_close_result(period, success, message))


async def _notify_close_result(period: str, success: bool,
                               message: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """发送站内消息 + 短信。"""
    from app.clients.m17.m03_client import M03Client
    m03 = M03Client()
    template = "M17_PERIOD_CLOSE_OK" if success else "M17_PERIOD_CLOSE_FAIL"
    msg_id = await m03.send_message(
        template_code=template,
        params={"period": period, "message": message},
        biz_no="PE" + period.replace("-", ""))
    return {"period": period, "message_id": msg_id}
```

### 7.3 APScheduler 定时任务

| 任务名 | Cron | 说明 | 影响范围 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `job_m17_voucher_partition_prebuild` | `0 1 25 * *` | **每月 25 日预建下 3 个月凭证/分录/明细账分区** | 3 张分区表 |
| `job_m17_ledger_log_partition` | `0 1 1 * *` | 预建未来 3 个月审计日志分区，DETACH 超 36 个月分区 | `m17_ledger_log` |
| `job_m17_ledger_identity_check` | `0 */2 * * *` | **每 2 小时巡检会计恒等式**（凭证平衡/余额连续/明细合计/总账明细） | 全库 |
| `job_m17_ledger_pending_alert` | `0 9 * * *` | 待审核/待过账超 2 天的凭证告警 | 凭证 |
| `job_m17_close_remind` | `0 10 3-8 * *` | 每月 3~8 日提醒财务结上月账 | 期间 |
| `job_m17_close_notify_retry` | `*/5 * * * *` | **重试 M16 锁账回调**（`m16_notified = FALSE` 的期间） | `m17_period_close` |
| `job_m17_ledger_balance_yearly_reset` | `0 2 1 1 *` | 年初校验损益类科目期初是否已归零 | 余额表 |
| `job_m17_voucher_code_reconcile` | `0 3 * * *` | 凭证号计数器与 DB max 对账 | Redis 计数器 |
| `job_m17_ledger_ref_reconcile` | `30 3 * * *` | 悬挂引用检查（科目/部门/凭证） | 全表 |
| `job_m17_ledger_cache_warmup` | `0 7 * * *` | 预热当日工作台与期间状态缓存 | Redis |
| `job_m17_ledger_balance_snapshot` | `0 23 * * *` | 每日余额快照（供趋势分析） | 余额表 |
| `job_m17_close_stale_recovery` | `*/15 * * * *` | 恢复卡在 `CLOSING` 超 30 分钟的结账 | `m17_period_close` |

```python
# app/jobs/m17/ledger_jobs.py
"""M17-05 总账与期末结账 —— APScheduler 定时任务"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

from apscheduler.schedulers.asyncio import AsyncIOScheduler
from apscheduler.triggers.cron import CronTrigger
from apscheduler.triggers.interval import IntervalTrigger
from sqlalchemy import text

from app.core.database import AsyncSessionLocal
from app.core.redis import redis_client

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
scheduler = AsyncIOScheduler(timezone="Asia/Shanghai")

PARTITIONED_TABLES = ("m17_voucher", "m17_voucher_entry", "m17_ledger_detail")


async def prebuild_voucher_partitions() -> dict:
    """预建未来 3 个月的凭证相关分区（必须有 DEFAULT 分区兜底）。"""
    created = []
    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        for table in PARTITIONED_TABLES:
            for offset in range(3):
                first = (datetime.now().replace(day=1)
                         + timedelta(days=32 * (offset + 1))).replace(day=1)
                nxt = (first + timedelta(days=32)).replace(day=1)
                part = "%s_p%s" % (table, first.strftime("%Y%m"))
                try:
                    await db.execute(text(
                        "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS %s PARTITION OF %s "
                        "FOR VALUES FROM ('%s') TO ('%s')"
                        % (part, table, first.strftime("%Y-%m"),
                           nxt.strftime("%Y-%m"))))
                    created.append(part)
                except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
                    logger.warning("create partition failed %s: %s", part, exc)
        await db.commit()
    logger.info("prebuilt %d partitions", len(created))
    return {"created": created}


async def check_ledger_identity() -> dict:
    """巡检四条会计恒等式，任何违规都立即告警。

    由于四条恒等式都已由数据库 CHECK 强制，本巡检的目的不是"发现脏数据"，
    而是"确认约束仍然有效"——若巡检发现问题，说明约束被误删或数据被绕过写入。
    """
    issues = []
    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        # ① 凭证级借贷平衡
        bad_vouchers = (await db.execute(text(
            "SELECT count(*) FROM m17_voucher "
            "WHERE debit_total <> credit_total"))).scalar() or 0
        if bad_vouchers:
            issues.append({"check": "VOUCHER_BALANCE", "count": bad_vouchers})

        # ② 余额连续性（借方科目）
        bad_debit = (await db.execute(text(
            "SELECT count(*) FROM m17_ledger_balance "
            "WHERE balance_direction = 'DEBIT' "
            "  AND end_balance <> open_balance + period_debit - period_credit"
        ))).scalar() or 0
        if bad_debit:
            issues.append({"check": "BALANCE_CONTINUITY_DEBIT", "count": bad_debit})

        # ③ 余额连续性（贷方科目）
        bad_credit = (await db.execute(text(
            "SELECT count(*) FROM m17_ledger_balance "
            "WHERE balance_direction = 'CREDIT' "
            "  AND end_balance <> open_balance + period_credit - period_debit"
        ))).scalar() or 0
        if bad_credit:
            issues.append({"check": "BALANCE_CONTINUITY_CREDIT", "count": bad_credit})

        # ④ 明细合计 vs 凭证头
        bad_entries = (await db.execute(text(
            """
            SELECT count(*) FROM m17_voucher v
            JOIN (
                SELECT voucher_id, period,
                       SUM(CASE WHEN dc_flag='D' THEN amount ELSE 0 END) dr,
                       SUM(CASE WHEN dc_flag='C' THEN amount ELSE 0 END) cr
                  FROM m17_voucher_entry GROUP BY voucher_id, period
            ) e ON e.voucher_id = v.id AND e.period = v.period
            WHERE e.dr <> v.debit_total OR e.cr <> v.credit_total
            """
        ))).scalar() or 0
        if bad_entries:
            issues.append({"check": "ENTRY_TOTAL", "count": bad_entries})

        # ⑤ 已过账凭证必须锁定
        unlocked = (await db.execute(text(
            "SELECT count(*) FROM m17_voucher "
            "WHERE status IN ('POSTED','REVERSED') AND is_locked = FALSE"
        ))).scalar() or 0
        if unlocked:
            issues.append({"check": "POSTED_LOCKED", "count": unlocked})

        # ⑥ 期间：已结账必须已回调 M16
        unnotified = (await db.execute(text(
            "SELECT count(*) FROM m17_period_close "
            "WHERE status <> 'CLOSING' AND m16_notified = FALSE"
        ))).scalar() or 0
        if unnotified:
            issues.append({"check": "PERIOD_M16_NOTIFIED", "count": unnotified})

    if issues:
        logger.critical("会计恒等式巡检发现异常：%s", issues)
        await redis_client.publish("m17:alert:identity_violation", str(issues))
    else:
        logger.info("会计恒等式巡检通过：四条恒等式全部成立")
    return {"ok": not issues, "issues": issues}


async def retry_close_notify() -> dict:
    """重试未成功的 M16 锁账回调。"""
    from app.tasks.m17.ledger_tasks import notify_m16_period_closed_task

    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        rows = (await db.execute(text(
            "SELECT period, m16_event_id, status FROM m17_period_close "
            "WHERE m16_notified = FALSE "
            "  AND created_at > CURRENT_TIMESTAMP - INTERVAL '7 days' "
            "ORDER BY period"
        ))).fetchall()

    for r in rows:
        action = "REOPEN" if r.status == "REOPENED" else "CLOSE"
        notify_m16_period_closed_task.delay(r.period, r.m16_event_id or "", action)
    return {"retried": len(rows)}


async def recover_stale_closing() -> dict:
    """恢复卡在 CLOSING 超 30 分钟的结账（进程崩溃等场景）。"""
    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        rows = (await db.execute(text(
            "SELECT period FROM m17_period_close "
            "WHERE status = 'CLOSING' "
            "  AND updated_at < CURRENT_TIMESTAMP - INTERVAL '30 minutes'"
        ))).scalars().all()
    for period in rows:
        logger.warning("recovering stale CLOSING period %s", period)
        await redis_client.delete("m17:ledger:close_lock:%s" % period)
    return {"recovered": len(rows)}


async def alert_pending_vouchers() -> dict:
    """待审核/待过账超 2 天的凭证告警。"""
    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        pending = (await db.execute(text(
            "SELECT period, count(*) FROM m17_voucher "
            "WHERE status IN ('SUBMITTED','AUDITED') "
            "  AND maker_at < CURRENT_TIMESTAMP - INTERVAL '2 days' "
            "GROUP BY period"
        ))).fetchall()
    for row in pending:
        logger.warning("period %s has %d pending vouchers > 2 days",
                       row.period, row.count)
    if pending:
        await redis_client.publish("m17:alert:pending_vouchers", str(len(pending)))
    return {"periods": len(pending)}


scheduler.add_job(prebuild_voucher_partitions,
                  CronTrigger(day=25, hour=1, minute=0),
                  id="job_m17_voucher_partition_prebuild",
                  name="预建凭证分区", replace_existing=True)
scheduler.add_job(check_ledger_identity, IntervalTrigger(hours=2),
                  id="job_m17_ledger_identity_check",
                  name="会计恒等式巡检", replace_existing=True)
scheduler.add_job(retry_close_notify, IntervalTrigger(minutes=5),
                  id="job_m17_close_notify_retry",
                  name="M16 锁账回调重试", replace_existing=True)
scheduler.add_job(recover_stale_closing, IntervalTrigger(minutes=15),
                  id="job_m17_close_stale_recovery",
                  name="结账僵死恢复", replace_existing=True)
scheduler.add_job(alert_pending_vouchers, CronTrigger(hour=9, minute=0),
                  id="job_m17_ledger_pending_alert",
                  name="待办凭证告警", replace_existing=True)
```

---

## 8. 业务规则与校验

### 8.1 编码规则

| 对象 | 规则 | 示例 | 冲突处理 |
|---|---|---|---|
| **记账凭证** | `V` + `YYYYMM` + `-` + 4 位流水 | `V202610-0001` | Redis `INCR` + 唯一索引兜底，重试 3 次后 `"1705040"` |
| **冲销凭证** | `RV` + `YYYYMM` + `-` + 4 位流水 | `RV202610-0001` | 同上 |
| **结账凭证** | `PV` + `YYYYMM` | `PV202610` | 期间唯一（一个期间一张结账凭证） |
| 会计期间 | `PE` + `YYYYMM` | `PE202610` | 由 M17-01 维护 |
| 审计日志号 | `LL` + `YYYYMMDD` + 8 位 | `LL20261031000123` | Redis `INCR` |

**规则要点：**

1. **凭证号在草稿创建时即分配**（不等到过账），便于财务沟通与跟踪。
2. **作废凭证不回退号段**：`V202610-0521` 作废后，下一个号码是 `-0522`，保证号段连续。
3. **冲销凭证用独立 `RV` 前缀**：让「原凭证 / 冲销凭证」在单号上一眼可辨。
4. **结账凭证 `PV` 每期唯一**：由 `m17_period_close.closing_voucher_no` 与期间一对一约束保证。
5. **单号生成失败不得降级为随机数**：直接抛错由调用方重试（`"1705040"` 表示并发冲突已重试仍失败）。

**违反时错误码：** `"1705040"`（并发冲突）、`"1705026"`（月流水耗尽 > 9999）。

### 8.2 唯一性规则

| 校验 | 规则 | 唯一键 | 违反时错误码 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 凭证号唯一 | 同一期间内唯一 | `uk_m17_voucher_no (voucher_no, period)` | `"1705040"` |
| 分录行号唯一 | 同一凭证内唯一 | `uk_m17_voucher_entry_no (voucher_id, entry_no, period)` | `"1705040"` |
| **明细账幂等** | 一条分录只生成一条明细账 | `uk_m17_ledger_detail_entry (entry_id, period)` | 过账幂等（重复过账被拒） |
| **余额行唯一** | 科目×期间×辅助核算唯一 | `uk_m17_ledger_balance_subject` | `"1705010"` |
| **总账唯一** | 一级科目×期间唯一 | `uk_m17_ledger_general` | `"1705011"` |
| 试算轮次唯一 | 期间×轮次唯一 | `uk_m17_trial_balance_round` | `"1705012"` |
| **期间结账唯一** | 一个期间一条结账记录 | `uk_m17_period_close_period (period, org_id)` | `"1705030"` |
| 检查项唯一 | 结账×检查项唯一 | `uk_m17_period_close_check` | 覆盖更新 |
| 年结唯一 | 一年度一条 | `uk_m17_year_close_year` | `"1705031"` |
| 日志号唯一 | 日志流水唯一 | `uk_m17_ledger_log_no` | `"1705040"` |

> **关键：余额行与总账行的唯一性是财务正确性的基础。** 若余额行重复，同一科目的余额会被算两次，试算必然不平。因此辅助核算用 `'NONE'` 哨兵值（而非 NULL）保证唯一索引生效（详见数据结构 4.5 设计说明）。

### 8.3 状态流转规则

**（1）凭证状态流转（见 3.2（1），此处列校验要点）**

| 当前状态 | 动作 | 目标状态 | 校验要点 | 违反时错误码 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| （无） | 新建 | `DRAFT` | 期间 `OPEN` + 借贷平衡 + 末级科目 | `"1705003"` / `"1705001"` / `"1705011"` |
| `DRAFT` | 提交 | `SUBMITTED` | 借贷平衡 + 分录 ≥2 | `"1705001"` |
| `SUBMITTED` | 审核通过 | `AUDITED` | **审核人 ≠ 制单人** + 借贷平衡 + 末级科目 + 期间 `OPEN` | `"1705010"` / `"1705001"` / `"1705011"` / `"1705003"` |
| `SUBMITTED`/`AUDITED` | 驳回 | `DRAFT` | 必填原因 ≥5 字 | `"1705007"` |
| `AUDITED` | 过账 | `POSTED` | 期间 `OPEN` + **过账人 ≠ 制单人** + 明细合计一致 + 末级科目 + 上期已结账 | `"1705004"` / `"1705010"` / `"1705002"` / `"1705011"` / `"1705020"` |
| `POSTED` | 红冲 | （原凭证）`REVERSED` | 未被冲销 + 原因 ≥5 字 + 期间 `OPEN` | `"1705008"` / `"1705035"` / `"1705023"` / `"1705003"` |
| `DRAFT`/`SUBMITTED` | 作废 | `CANCELLED` | 原因 ≥5 字 | `"1705009"` |
| `POSTED`/`REVERSED`/`CANCELLED` | **任何修改** | — | **物理禁止**（REVOKE + 触发器） | `"1705005"` |

**（2）会计期间状态流转**

| 当前状态 | 动作 | 目标状态 | 校验要点 | 违反时错误码 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `NOT_OPEN` | 启用 | `OPEN` | 由 M17-01 执行 | — |
| `OPEN` | 月末结账 | `CLOSED` | 试算平衡 + 12 项检查通过 + 无未过账 + 上期已结 + 二次确认 | `"1705012"` / `"1705013"` / `"1705014"` / `"1705020"` / `"1705025"` |
| `CLOSED` | 反结账 | `OPEN` | 审批单号 + 后续期间未结账 + 未超不可反结天数 + 原因 | `"1705022"` / `"1705032"` / `"1705033"` / `"1705023"` |
| `CLOSED` | 年结 | `LOCKED` | 12 期全部结账 + M16 全部锁账 | `"1705021"` / `"1705024"` |
| `LOCKED` | 反年结 | `CLOSED` | 院长审批单号 + 原因 ≥10 字 | `"1705031"` / `"1705022"` / `"1705023"` |

**（3）结账状态流转（含 M16 回调）**

| 当前状态 | 动作 | 目标状态 | 校验要点 | 违反时错误码 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| （无） | 执行结账 | `CLOSING` | 7 步前置校验全通过 | `"1705003"` 等 |
| `CLOSING` | M16 回调成功 | `CLOSED` | `m16_notified = TRUE` | — |
| `CLOSING` | M16 回调失败 | `CLOSING`（保持） | 每 5 分钟重试 | `"1705024"` |
| `CLOSED` | 反结账 | `REOPENED` | 审批 + 后续未结 + 未超期 | `"1705022"` 等 |
| `REOPENED` | 重新结账 | `CLOSING` → `CLOSED` | 同上 | — |
| `CLOSED`/`LOCKED` | 直接置 `CLOSING` | — | **数据库 CHECK 拒绝**（`m16_notified` 不一致） | `"1705024"` |

### 8.4 业务校验规则（分场景）

**（1）凭证录入与修改**

| 规则 | 说明 | 错误码 |
|---|---|---|
| 期间必须 `OPEN` | `CLOSED`/`LOCKED` 不可录凭证 | `"1705003"` |
| 凭证日期必须落在期间内 | `to_char(voucher_date,'YYYY-MM') = period` | `"1705001"` |
| **借贷必须平衡** | `Σ借 = Σ贷` | `"1705001"` |
| 分录数 ≥ 2 | 单边凭证无意义 | `"1705001"` |
| 分录行号从 1 连续 | 不可跳号或重复 | `40001` |
| 金额必须为正 | 红冲用方向反转，不用负数 | `40001` |
| 金额精确到分 | 最多 2 位小数 | `40001` |
| 科目必须存在且为末级 | 非末级科目不可记账 | `"1705013"` / `"1705011"` |
| 辅助核算成对 | `aux_type` 与 `aux_code` 同时有值 | `40001` |
| `DEPT` 辅助核算必须有 `dept_id` | — | `40001` |
| 业务凭证不可手工修改 | `voucher_source ∈ {REVENUE, COST, DEPT, BANK}` 只读 | `"1705034"` |

**（2）审核与过账**

| 规则 | 说明 | 错误码 |
|---|---|---|
| **职责分离：审核人 ≠ 制单人** | 内控要求 | `"1705010"` |
| **职责分离：过账人 ≠ 制单人** | 内控要求 | `"1705010"` |
| 审核时状态必须 `SUBMITTED` | — | `"1705006"` |
| 驳回必须填原因 ≥5 字 | — | `"1705007"` |
| 过账时状态必须 `AUDITED` | — | `"1705004"` |
| **明细合计 = 凭证头合计** | 过账前必查 | `"1705002"` |
| 过账时期间必须 `OPEN` | — | `"1705003"` |
| **上期必须已结账** | 不可跳期过账 | `"1705020"` |
| 全部分录为末级科目 | — | `"1705011"` |
| 批量过账默认整批回滚 | 保证一致性 | `"1705028"` |

**（3）红冲与作废**

| 规则 | 说明 | 错误码 |
|---|---|---|
| 仅 `POSTED` 可红冲 | 未过账直接作废即可 | `"1705008"` |
| **不可重复冲销** | `reversed_by_voucher_id` 已存在 | `"1705035"` |
| 冲销原因 ≥5 字 | 必须说明为什么冲 | `"1705023"` |
| 冲销期间必须 `OPEN` | — | `"1705003"` |
| 冲销凭证同样受借贷平衡约束 | 数据库 CHECK | `"1705001"` |
| 红冲不修改原凭证内容 | 只改状态 + 回填 `reversed_by_voucher_id` | — |
| **仅 `DRAFT`/`SUBMITTED` 可作废** | `AUDITED` 及以上不可作废 | `"1705009"` |
| 作废原因 ≥5 字 | — | `40001` |

**（4）结账与年结**

| 规则 | 说明 | 错误码 |
|---|---|---|
| **试算必须平衡** | 三组借贷合计相等 | `"1705012"` |
| **无未过账凭证** | `SUBMITTED`/`AUDITED` 数量 = 0 | `"1705014"` |
| **全部 FAIL 级检查通过** | 7 项 FAIL 检查项 | `"1705013"` |
| **上期已结账** | 不可跳期结账 | `"1705020"` |
| 期间状态 `OPEN` | — | `"1705003"` |
| **必须二次确认** | `confirm = true` | `"1705025"` |
| **WARN 豁免必须有审批单号** | — | `"1705025"` |
| 必须生成结账凭证 | `closing_voucher_no` 非空 | `"1705036"` |
| **必须回调 M16 锁账** | 未回调不算结完 | `"1705024"` |
| 年结要求 12 期全部结账 | — | `"1705021"` |
| 年结要求 12 期 M16 全部锁账 | — | `"1705024"` |
| **净利润 = 收入 − 费用** | 数据库 CHECK | `"1705031"` |

**（5）反结账与反年结**

| 规则 | 说明 | 错误码 |
|---|---|---|
| 必须有审批单号 | 反结账（M20）、反年结（院长） | `"1705022"` |
| 反结账原因 ≥5 字 | — | `"1705023"` |
| 反年结原因 ≥10 字 | 更高风险需更详细说明 | `"1705023"` |
| **不可跳期反结账** | 后续期间必须先反结账 | `"1705032"` |
| **反结账有时间限制** | 默认 30 天内（可配） | `"1705033"` |
| 反年结仅限 `LOCKED` 年度 | — | `"1705031"` |
| 反结账/反年结必须二次确认 | — | `"1705025"` |
| 反结账必须通知 M16 重开期间 | `m17.period.reopened` 事件 | `"1705024"` |

### 8.5 计算规则

> ⚠️ **本章全部是账簿层面的算术关系**，不产生新的业务金额。原始金额来自上游（M16 日结、M14/M15 成本），本模块只做归集、结转与平衡校验。

**（1）核心公式汇总**

| 计算项 | 公式 | 舍入 | 强制位置 |
|---|---|---|---|
| **凭证借贷平衡** | `Σ(amount WHERE dc_flag='D') = Σ(amount WHERE dc_flag='C')` | 精确到分 | `ck_m17_voucher_balance` |
| **借方科目期末余额** | `end = open + period_debit − period_credit` | `ROUND_HALF_UP` 到 0.01 | `ck_m17_ledger_balance_debit` |
| **贷方科目期末余额** | `end = open + period_credit − period_debit` | 同上 | `ck_m17_ledger_balance_credit` |
| **逐笔余额（借方科目）** | `running = prev + (D ? +amount : −amount)` | 同上 | 服务层计算 |
| **逐笔余额（贷方科目）** | `running = prev + (C ? +amount : −amount)` | 同上 | 服务层计算 |
| **试算平衡（三组）** | `Σopen_debit = Σopen_credit`；`Σperiod_debit = Σperiod_credit`；`Σend_debit = Σend_credit` | 同上 | `ck_m17_trial_balance_*` |
| **总账期末余额** | 同科目余额公式（一级科目汇总） | 同上 | `ck_m17_ledger_general_*` |
| **总账 vs 明细账** | `end_balance = leaf_balance_sum` | 同上 | `ck_m17_ledger_general_balanced` |
| **年末净利润** | `net_profit = profit_income_total − profit_expense_total` | 同上 | `ck_m17_year_close_net_profit` |
| **损益结转金额** | `Σ(PL_INCOME 期末余额)` 与 `Σ(PL_EXPENSE 期末余额)` | 同上 | 结账服务 |
| **本年累计** | 1 月 = 本月；2 月起 = 上月 `ytd` + 本月 | 同上 | 过账服务 |
| 结账完成度 | 5 项前置 + 12 项检查 | 枚举 | 结账服务 |

**（2）标准算例**

**算例 A · 凭证借贷平衡（门诊日结 128,650.00 元）**

```
借 1001 库存现金                15,300.00
借 1002 银行存款               108,450.00
借 1122 应收账款-医保            5,200.00
借 6602 财务费用-手续费            300.00
贷 4001 医疗收入-门诊          129,250.00
────────────────────────────────────────
借方合计 129,250.00  =  贷方合计 129,250.00  ✓ 平衡
```

**算例 B · 借方科目余额（1002 银行存款，2026-10）**

```
期初 2,000,000.00
本期借方 3,450,000.00
本期贷方 2,780,000.00
期末 = 2,000,000.00 + 3,450,000.00 − 2,780,000.00 = 2,670,000.00
（走 ck_m17_ledger_balance_debit）
```

**算例 C · 贷方科目余额（2202 应付账款，2026-10）**

```
期初 860,000.00
本期贷方 420,000.00（新增应付）
本期借方 310,000.00（付款）
期末 = 860,000.00 + 420,000.00 − 310,000.00 = 970,000.00
（走 ck_m17_ledger_balance_credit）
```

**算例 D · 试算平衡（2026-10）**

```
组次        借方合计          贷方合计          差额
① 期初    12,480,000.00    12,480,000.00      0.00
② 本期发生  8,620,450.00     8,620,450.00      0.00
③ 期末    15,320,700.00    15,320,700.00      0.00
→ is_balanced = TRUE
```

**算例 E · 损益结转（月末结账）**

```
收入类：4001 医疗收入 1,286,500.00 + 4002 药品收入 342,800.00 = 1,629,300.00
费用类：5001 医疗业务成本 862,400.00 + 5101 管理费用 215,600.00 = 1,078,000.00
净利润 = 1,629,300.00 − 1,078,000.00 = 551,300.00

结账凭证 PV202610：
  借 4001 医疗收入          1,286,500.00
  借 4002 药品收入            342,800.00
  贷 3103 本年利润          1,629,300.00
  借 3103 本年利润          1,078,000.00
  贷 5001 医疗业务成本        862,400.00
  贷 5101 管理费用            215,600.00
  ──────────────────────────────────────
  借方合计 2,707,300.00 = 贷方合计 2,707,300.00 ✓
```

**算例 F · 年结结转（2026 年度）**

```
全年损益收入 18,426,500.00
全年损益费用 15,238,400.00
本年净利润 = 18,426,500.00 − 15,238,400.00 = 3,188,100.00（盈利）

年结凭证：
  借 3103 本年利润              3,188,100.00
  贷 3104 利润分配-未分配利润    3,188,100.00

次年 2027-01 期初：
  1002 银行存款   = 2026-12 期末（延续）
  4001 医疗收入   = 0.00（归零）
  3104 未分配利润 = 2026-12 期末 + 3,188,100.00
```

**（3）舍入规则**

| 场景 | 规则 |
|---|---|
| 金额计算 | `ROUND_HALF_UP` 到 0.01（`Decimal.quantize(Decimal("0.01"))`） |
| 结转为零判定 | 金额绝对值 < 0.005 视为 0.00 |
| 比率计算 | `NUMERIC(9,6)`，6 位小数 |
| **禁止** | 使用 `float` / `double` 做任何金额运算（一律用 `Decimal`） |

> **尾差铁律（共享规范 §2.4）：** 按比例分摊的金额，最后一项必须吸收尾差，保证 `Σ分摊额 = 待分摊总额`（精确到分）。本模块的损益结转不涉及分摊，但过账时的余额累加必须严格用 `Decimal`，避免浮点误差累积导致试算不平。

### 8.6 引用与删除规则

| 对象 | 删除方式 | 约束 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| **会计凭证** | **物理禁止删除** | `REVOKE DELETE` + 触发器 | 只能作废（未过账）或红冲（已过账） |
| **凭证明细** | **物理禁止删除** | `REVOKE DELETE` + 触发器 | 随凭证一起不可删 |
| **明细账** | **永不允许删除** | 只增不改 | 过账时生成，永不修改 |
| 科目余额 | 不允许删除 | 只更新 | 无发生的科目保留余额行 |
| 总账 | 不允许删除 | 只更新 | — |
| 试算平衡 | 不允许删除 | 只追加轮次 | 留证 |
| **期间结账记录** | **不允许删除** | 只能反结账 | 法定记录 |
| 结账检查项 | 不允许删除 | 随结账记录保留 | — |
| 年结记录 | 不允许删除 | 只能反年结 | 法定记录 |
| 审计日志 | 不允许删除 | 只追加，超期 `DETACH` 归档 | 36 个月在线 |

**引用检查规则：**

| 场景 | 处理 |
|---|---|
| 删除/停用科目时 | 若该科目在本期有余额或分录，拒绝（`"1705011"`），由 M17-01 校验 |
| 作废凭证时 | 已过账凭证不可作废（`"1705009"`） |
| 冲销凭证时 | 已冲销凭证不可重复冲销（`"1705035"`） |
| 反结账时 | 后续期间已结账则拒绝（`"1705032"`） |
| 结账时 | 存在未过账凭证则拒绝（`"1705014"`） |
| 删除期间时 | 该期间有任何凭证则拒绝（由 M17-01 校验） |

### 8.7 并发控制

| 场景 | 机制 | 实现 | 冲突返回 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 凭证编辑 | 乐观锁 `version` | `UPDATE ... WHERE version = :v` | `40900` |
| **凭证过账** | Redis 幂等键 + 数据库状态机 | `m17:idem:ledger:post:{key}` + `status = 'AUDITED'` 条件更新 | `"1705004"`（状态已变） |
| **结账** | **会计期间行锁** | `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE` on `m17_period_close` + Redis 锁 `m17:ledger:close_lock:{period}` | `"1705030"` |
| 反结账 | 同上 | 同上 | `"1705030"` |
| 余额更新 | `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE` + 乐观锁 `version` | 按 `(subject_code, period, aux)` 行锁 | 重试 3 次后 `"1705010"` |
| 凭证号生成 | Redis `INCR` + 唯一索引 | 原子递增 + 冲突捕获重试 | `"1705040"` |
| 试算平衡 | 无锁（只读 + 落库） | 允许并发多轮，`round_no` 递增 | — |
| 年结 | 年度行锁 | `FOR UPDATE` on `m17_year_close` | `"1705030"` |
| 批量过账 | 分批 + 每批独立事务（异步模式） | 批内单事务，批间顺序 | 失败的批不影响已成功批 |

**并发控制要点：**

1. **结账必须用悲观锁（`FOR UPDATE`）而非乐观锁。** 结账要遍历全部凭证、生成结账凭证、结转上千个科目，事务持续时间较长。若用乐观锁，两个并发结账可能在最后一步才发现冲突，导致大量无效工作。悲观锁在一开始就互斥，代价是等待，但语义正确。

2. **过账用「状态条件更新」而非纯乐观锁。** `UPDATE m17_voucher SET status='POSTED' WHERE voucher_no=? AND status='AUDITED'`——若 `rowcount = 0` 说明状态已被他人改变，直接返回 `"1705004"`。这比读版本号再比更简洁可靠。

3. **余额更新必须行锁。** 两张凭证同时过账且涉及同一科目时，若不用行锁，两个事务都读到旧余额再各自写新余额，后写的会覆盖先写的，导致余额少算——而由于每张凭证自身借贷平衡，凭证级 CHECK 发现不了这个错误，只有试算平衡会暴露。这是财务系统最隐蔽的并发 bug，必须用 `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE` 避免。

4. **凭证号冲突的兜底是唯一索引而非 Redis。** Redis `INCR` 保证不重复，但 Redis 可能重启丢失计数器。因此数据库 `uk_m17_voucher_no` 是最终防线：捕获唯一约束冲突后，重新取号并重试（最多 3 次）。

5. **批量过账的幂等。** 异步批量过账可能因网络重试被提交两次。通过「凭证状态条件更新」天然幂等：第二次执行时凭证已是 `POSTED`，条件不满足，不会重复生成明细账。此外 `uk_m17_ledger_detail_entry` 唯一索引兜底。
